Opera Mineralia, sive de Lapide Philosophico (Isaac Hollandus, 1600)
==========================================================
The collected mineral works of Isaac Hollandus on the philosophers' stone; the Dutch practical alchemy of the late 16th century. Public domain. Digitized from Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/b32988382).
